                  ⋯)

theorem Research.DerivativeSwitch.ActualBoundaryCup.traced_right_boundary.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5, u_6} : ∀
  {R : Type u_1} {L0 : Type u_2} {L1 : Type u_3} {L2 : Type u_4} {T1 : Type u_5} {T2 : Type u_6} [inst : CommRing R]
  [inst_1 : AddCommGroup L0]
  [inst_2 :
    @Module R L0 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid L0 inst_1)]
  [inst_3 : AddCommGroup L1]
  [inst_4 :
    @Module R L1 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid L1 inst_3)]
  [inst_5 : AddCommGroup L2]
  [inst_6 :
    @Module R L2 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid L2 inst_5)]
  [inst_7 : AddCommGroup T1]
  [inst_8 :
    @Module R T1 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T1 inst_7)]
  [inst_9 : AddCommGroup T2]
  [inst_10 :
    @Module R T2 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T2 inst_9)]
  (D :
    @Research.DerivativeSwitch.ActualBoundaryCup.LocalCupData R L0 L1 L2 T1 T2 inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 inst_5
      inst_6 inst_7 inst_8 inst_9 inst_10)
  (trace :
    @LinearMap R R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
      (@RingHom.id R (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)))) T2
      R (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T2 inst_9)
      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))) inst_10
      (@Semiring.toModule R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)))),
  (∀ (x : T1),
      @Eq R
        (@DFunLike.coe
          (@LinearMap R R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
            (@RingHom.id R
              (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))))
            T2 R (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T2 inst_9)
            (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))) inst_10

            (@Semiring.toModule R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)))

              (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)))))
          trace
          (@DFunLike.coe
            (@LinearMap R R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
              (@RingHom.id R
                (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))))
              T1 T2 (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T1 inst_7) (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T2 inst_9) inst_8 inst_10)
            T1 (fun x => T2)
            (@LinearMap.instFunLike R R T1 T2 (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)) (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T1 inst_7)
              (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid T2 inst_9) inst_8 inst_10
              (@RingHom.id R
                (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst)))))
            (@Research.DerivativeSwitch.ActualBoundaryCup.LocalCupData.dt R L0 L1 L2 T1 T2 inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3
              inst_4 inst_5 inst_6 inst_7 inst_8 inst_9 inst_10 D)
            x))
        (@OfNat.ofNat R (nat_lit 0)
          (@Zero.toOfNat0 R
            (@MulZeroClass.toZero R
              (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))))))) →
    ∀ (l : L1) (q : L0),
      @Eq L2
          (@DFunLike.coe
            (@LinearMap R R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))
              (@RingHom.id R
                (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring R (@CommSemiring.toSemiring R (@CommRing.toCommSemiring R inst))))
